- **brave-search-api-422-is-ambiguous-read-f981e94986df1701**: Never classify a Brave Search API failure by HTTP status alone: - 422 + error.code SUBSCRIPTION_TOKEN_INVALID: the key is wrong or revoked. Rotate the key. - 422 + error.code VALIDATION: a parameter is wrong (e.g. an invalid country code). Fix the parameter. - 400 + error.code OPTION_NOT_IN_PLAN: your plan lacks that feature. Upgrade or drop the option. - 429 + error.code RATE_LIMITED: too many requests this second. Cool down and retry.
